Agent MechSuits: Mechanistic Subspace Safety Steering for Multi-Turn CLI Agents
Authors: Weidi Luo, Qiming Zhang, Yihao Quan, Mingyu Jin, Jie Cai, Chaowei Xiao, Jingcheng Niu, Zhen Xiang
Venue: NeurIPS
First: 2026-06-21T21:13:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-05T17:21:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted by NeurIPS'2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Command-Line Interface (CLI) agents based on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable autonomous capabilities, but they also introduce significant safety and misuse risks during multi-turn interactions with external environments. Existing safety mechanisms mainly rely on external guardrails, which have a limited ability to perform fine-grained behavioral control during execution. Meanwhile, recent mechanistic interpretability methods for LLM safety are mostly confined to single-turn or jailbreak-style QA settings, limiting their ability to capture the evolving risk dynamics of multi-turn agent execution. In this paper, we investigate the safety of multi-turn CLI agents from an internal perspective. We propose Agent MechSuits (Mechanistic Subspace Intervention and Steering), a white-box defense framework that performs runtime safety detection and representation-level mitigation for CLI agents. Unlike conventional agent guardrails, Agent MechSuits detects harmful execution states from step-level hidden representations and mitigates unsafe behavior by intervening in a 10-dimensional subspace within a single layer. To support this research, we introduce the Mechanistic Agent Safety (MAS) benchmark, comprising comprehensively annotated multi-turn execution trajectories across 194 tasks using LLaMA-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, and Gemma-2-9B. Extensive experiments show that Agent MechSuits achieves strong safety detection performance, provides preliminary evidence for lookahead risk anticipation, and substantially reduces harmful actions of the CLI agent, establishing a foundation for applying mechanistic interpretability to dynamic LLM agent safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理机械套装：多轮CLI代理的机械子空间安全引导
基于大型语言模型（LLM）的命令行界面（CLI）代理展示了显著的自主能力，但在与外部环境的多轮交互中也引入了重大安全和误用风险。现有的安全机制主要依赖外部护栏，在执行过程中进行细粒度行为控制的能力有限。同时，最近的LLM安全机械解释方法大多局限于单轮或越狱式问答设置，限制了其捕捉多轮代理执行中不断变化的风险动态的能力。本文从内部视角研究多轮CLI代理的安全性。我们提出了代理机械套装（Agent MechSuits），这是一个白盒防御框架，能够对CLI代理进行运行时安全检测和表示级别的缓解。与传统的代理护栏不同，代理机械套装通过检测步骤级隐藏表示中的有害执行状态，并通过在单层内的10维子空间进行干预来缓解不安全行为。为了支持这项研究，我们引入了机械代理安全（MAS）基准，涵盖了使用LLaMA-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B和Gemma-2-9B的194个任务的全面注释的多轮执行轨迹。大量实验表明，代理机械套装在安全检测性能上表现出色，为前瞻性风险预判提供了初步证据，并显著减少了CLI代理的有害行为，为将机械解释应用于动态LLM代理安全奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the safety and misuse risks associated with multi-turn Command-Line Interface (CLI) agents powered by large language models (LLMs), which have shown impressive autonomous capabilities but are limited by existing safety mechanisms that primarily rely on external guardrails. These traditional methods struggle with fine-grained behavioral control and are inadequate for the evolving risk dynamics present in multi-turn interactions. The proposed approach, Agent MechSuits, introduces a white-box defense framework that detects harmful execution states and mitigates unsafe behavior by intervening in a 10-dimensional subspace of hidden representations during runtime, thus offering a more internal perspective on safety. This paper contributes by establishing the Mechanistic Agent Safety (MAS) benchmark, which includes annotated multi-turn execution trajectories across 194 tasks, and demonstrates that Agent MechSuits significantly improves safety detection performance and reduces harmful actions, thereby supporting the goal of enhancing LLM agent safety.
本研究关注由大型语言模型（LLM）驱动的多轮命令行界面（CLI）代理的安全性和误用风险，强调现有安全机制主要依赖外部护栏，无法有效进行执行过程中的细粒度行为控制。所提出的方法Agent MechSuits与传统方法不同，提供了一种白盒防御框架，通过检测隐藏表示中的有害执行状态，并通过在单层内的10维子空间进行干预来减轻不安全行为。该方法的动机明确，旨在增强多轮交互的安全性，论文的贡献在于引入了机制代理安全（MAS）基准，其中包括194个任务的注释执行轨迹。实验结果表明，Agent MechSuits在安全检测性能上表现出色，并显著减少了CLI代理的有害行为，从而支持了提高动态LLM代理交互安全性的目标。
All you need to break LLMs are Black-Box, Adapting, Efficient, Transferable, Harmful, Applicable ... Attacks
Authors: Vincent Limbach, Jonas Dornbusch, David Lüdke, Stephan Günnemann, Leo Schwinn
First: 2026-06-02T13:39:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-02T12:35:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurately evaluating adversarial robustness is a longstanding challenge. A flawed attack design can inflate robustness estimates, making deployment risk assessment and defense comparison unreliable. Historically, standardized attacks such as AutoAttack have largely resolved this for image classifiers, providing a reliable evaluation baseline for systematic comparison across defenses. However, no equivalent exists for LLM jailbreak evaluation yet, where designing such an attack is considerably more difficult. A reliable attack must, among other things, be black-box compatible, applicable to arbitrary defense pipelines, and efficient, which no existing method jointly satisfies. We introduce Indirect Harmfulness Optimization (IHO), a masked diffusion language model attacker trained via iterative preference optimization against a harmfulness judge, requiring only black-box access to the target. The same method can be used without modification as a strong adapting attack on individual behaviors, or as an efficient amortized policy that transfers to held-out behaviors and unseen target models without fine-tuning. Even against layered defenses, such as GLM 5.3-flash served through a black-box API deploying an auxiliary detector, IHO improves attack success considerably over state-of-the-art approaches, without any defense-specific adaptation. Our results position IHO as a practical step toward the kind of standardized jailbreak evaluation that has improved reliability in the past. Code and models are available on GitHub and Hugging Face.
中文标题/摘要
标题：破解LLM所需的仅仅是黑箱、适应性、有效、可转移、有害、适用……攻击
准确评估对抗鲁棒性一直是一个长期挑战。设计不当的攻击可能会夸大鲁棒性估计，使得部署风险评估和防御比较不可靠。历史上，标准化攻击如AutoAttack在图像分类器中基本解决了这个问题，为系统比较提供了可靠的评估基准。然而，目前尚无等效的LLM越狱评估方法，设计这样的攻击要困难得多。一个可靠的攻击必须在许多方面兼容黑箱，适用于任意防御管道，并且高效，而现有方法没有一个能够共同满足这些要求。我们引入了间接有害性优化（IHO），这是一种通过对抗有害性评估者进行迭代偏好优化训练的掩蔽扩散语言模型攻击者，仅需对目标进行黑箱访问。相同的方法可以在不修改的情况下作为对个体行为的强适应性攻击，或作为一种高效的摊销策略，能够转移到保留行为和未见目标模型上，而无需微调。即使面对分层防御，例如通过黑箱API部署辅助检测器的GLM 5.3-flash，IHO在攻击成功率上显著超越了最先进的方法，而无需任何特定于防御的适应。我们的结果将IHO定位为朝着标准化越狱评估迈出的实用一步，这在过去提高了可靠性。代码和模型可在GitHub和Hugging Face上获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of accurately evaluating adversarial robustness in large language models (LLMs), highlighting that existing attack designs can lead to inflated robustness estimates and unreliable defense comparisons. Previous methods, such as standardized attacks for image classifiers, lack an equivalent for LLM jailbreak evaluation, where designing effective attacks is more complex due to the need for black-box compatibility and efficiency. The proposed Indirect Harmfulness Optimization (IHO) method overcomes these limitations by utilizing a masked diffusion language model attacker trained through iterative preference optimization, allowing it to function effectively without requiring modifications for different behaviors or models. This paper contributes a novel approach that significantly improves attack success rates against layered defenses, demonstrating its effectiveness in providing a reliable evaluation baseline for LLMs, thus supporting the goal of standardized jailbreak evaluation.
本研究解决了准确评估大型语言模型（LLMs）对抗鲁棒性的问题，强调现有攻击设计可能导致鲁棒性估计膨胀，从而使风险评估和防御比较变得复杂。以往的方法，如针对图像分类器的标准化攻击，缺乏针对LLM越狱评估的等效方案，主要是由于设计有效攻击的复杂性，这些攻击需要具备黑箱兼容性和高效性。所提出的方法间接有害性优化（IHO）通过利用仅需黑箱访问的掩蔽扩散语言模型攻击者，克服了这些限制，使其既可以作为强大的适应性攻击，也可以作为高效的策略，转移到新的行为和模型。论文表明，IHO在分层防御下显著提高了攻击成功率，标志着朝着标准化越狱评估的重要进展，从而增强了对LLM鲁棒性的评估可靠性。
Scalable, Transferable Meta-network for Data Selection Requires a Different Loss (and Why the Obvious Choice is Problematic)
Authors: Zilin Du, Bowen Yang, Boyang Albert Li
First: 2026-10-01T17:23:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T17:23:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data selection is critical for training large language models on massive and heterogeneous corpora. Meta-learning for Training-data Selection offers a principled alternative to heuristic scoring by learning data weights from a target validation objective, but existing methods face a trade-off between fine-grained valuation and transferability to unseen data. A natural solution is to replace per-sample weights with a selection network. However, we find that directly incorporating such a network into existing MTS objectives leads to unstable optimization and poor generalization, caused by weight suppression and persistent reliance on easy-to-learn features. To address these issues, we propose Transferable Example Scoring and Selection (TESS), a scalable data-selection framework built on a Pointwise Value Matching objective (PVM). Experiments on LLM safety and targeted instruction tuning demonstrate strong transfer across datasets, from subsets to full corpora, and from smaller to larger models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可扩展的可转移元网络用于数据选择需要不同的损失（以及为什么明显的选择存在问题）
数据选择对于在大规模异构语料库上训练大型语言模型至关重要。用于训练数据选择的元学习提供了一种基于目标验证目标学习数据权重的原则性替代方案，但现有方法在细粒度评估和对未见数据的可转移性之间面临权衡。一个自然的解决方案是用选择网络替代每个样本的权重。然而，我们发现直接将这样的网络纳入现有的MTS目标会导致不稳定的优化和较差的泛化，原因在于权重抑制和对易学特征的持续依赖。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了可转移示例评分和选择（TESS），这是一个基于逐点值匹配目标（PVM）构建的可扩展数据选择框架。在LLM安全性和针对性指令调优的实验中，展示了从子集到完整语料库、从小模型到大模型的强转移。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the challenge of data selection for training large language models on diverse datasets, highlighting the limitations of existing meta-learning methods that struggle with balancing fine-grained data valuation and transferability to new data. The proposed approach, Transferable Example Scoring and Selection (TESS), introduces a scalable framework that utilizes a Pointwise Value Matching objective to overcome the instability and poor generalization associated with traditional methods that rely on per-sample weights. The contribution of this paper lies in its innovative methodology that enhances data selection efficiency and effectiveness, as demonstrated through experiments on LLM safety and targeted instruction tuning, which show strong transferability across various datasets and model sizes, thus supporting the goals of improved model training.
本研究解决了在多样化数据集上训练大型语言模型时的数据选择问题，强调了现有元学习方法在平衡细粒度数据评估和对新数据的可转移性方面的局限性。以往的方法往往导致优化不稳定和泛化能力差，因为它们依赖于容易学习的特征。所提出的方法——可转移示例评分与选择（TESS），引入了一种可扩展的框架，利用逐点价值匹配目标来克服这些问题，从而提高了稳定性和可转移性。本文贡献了一种新颖的数据选择方法，在与LLM安全性和目标指令调优相关的任务中显著提高了性能，展示了在不同数据集和模型规模之间的有效转移。
CHILLGuard: Towards Fine-Grained Chinese LLM Safety Guardrail with Scalable Data Construction and Model-aware Preference Alignment
Authors: Wenbo Yu, Bohua Wang, Hao Fang, Kuofeng Gao, Jingru Zeng, Xiaochen Yang, Tianyi Zhang, Xiaoxiao Ma, Jiawei Kong, Hao Wu, Bin Chen, Shu-Tao Xia, Min Zhang
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-06-13T16:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T13:45:00+00:00
Comments: accepted by EMNLP 2026 findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Malicious content generated from large language models (LLMs) could pose severe safety risks and ethical concerns. While existing LLM safety guardrails excel in English or multilingual settings, they lack adaptation to Chinese-specific regulatory policies, cultural context, and linguistic nuances, failing to support fine-grained risk classification for diverse deployment needs. In this paper, we introduce a 5-macro, 31-micro category fine-grained risk taxonomy for Chinese scenarios, and build CHILLGuard: a dedicated Chinese LLM content safety guardrail. To address the critical scarcity of high-quality annotated Chinese safety data, we propose a scalable multi-stage data construction pipeline: we expand multi-source corpus via retrieval-augmented generation, generate implicit harmful samples through prompt engineering rewriting, and refine high-quality data via multi-model voting-based label calibration. Based on this, we build CHILLGuardTrain, a large-scale training set with 405,007 samples, and CHILLGuardTest, a rigorously curated annotated test set with 51,745 samples. We then train CHILLGuard on CHILLGuardTrain under a generator-classifier collaborative framework via Model-aware Direct Preference Optimization. Extensive experiments under multiple settings demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of CHILLGuard, e.g., a 15.92% relative improvement of F1 score over Qwen3Guard-8B-Strict on our benchmark. We release our resources at https://github.com/cswbyu/CHILLGuard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CHILLGuard：面向细粒度中文LLM安全防护的可扩展数据构建与模型感知偏好对齐
大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的恶意内容可能带来严重的安全风险和伦理问题。现有的LLM安全防护在英语或多语言环境中表现出色，但缺乏对中国特定监管政策、文化背景和语言细微差别的适应，无法支持多样化部署需求的细粒度风险分类。本文介绍了一种针对中国场景的5个宏观类别和31个微观类别的细粒度风险分类法，并构建了CHILLGuard：一个专门的中文LLM内容安全防护。为了解决高质量标注中文安全数据的严重短缺，我们提出了一种可扩展的多阶段数据构建流程：通过检索增强生成扩展多源语料库，通过提示工程重写生成隐性有害样本，并通过多模型投票的标签校准精炼高质量数据。在此基础上，我们构建了CHILLGuardTrain，一个包含405,007个样本的大规模训练集，以及CHILLGuardTest，一个经过严格策划的包含51,745个样本的标注测试集。然后，我们在CHILLGuardTrain上通过模型感知直接偏好优化的生成器-分类器协作框架训练CHILLGuard。多种设置下的广泛实验表明，CHILLGuard的性能达到了最先进水平，例如，在我们的基准测试中，相较于Qwen3Guard-8B-Strict，F1分数提高了15.92%。我们在https://github.com/cswbyu/CHILLGuard发布我们的资源。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the safety risks and ethical concerns posed by malicious content generated from large language models (LLMs), particularly in the context of Chinese language and culture, where existing safety guardrails are inadequate. Previous methods have primarily focused on English or multilingual settings, lacking adaptation to Chinese-specific regulatory policies and linguistic nuances, which hinders fine-grained risk classification. This paper introduces CHILLGuard, a dedicated Chinese LLM content safety guardrail, supported by a scalable multi-stage data construction pipeline that includes retrieval-augmented generation and prompt engineering for harmful sample generation. The methodology involves training CHILLGuard on a large-scale dataset of 405,007 samples using a generator-classifier collaborative framework and Model-aware Direct Preference Optimization. The experimental results show that CHILLGuard achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.92% relative improvement in F1 score over existing models, thereby effectively supporting its goal of enhancing safety in Chinese LLM applications.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的恶意内容所带来的安全风险和伦理问题，特别是在中文语言和文化背景下，现有的安全防护措施显得不足。以往的方法主要集中在英语或多语言环境中，缺乏对中文特定监管政策和语言细微差别的适应性，限制了其在细粒度风险分类中的有效性。提出的方法CHILLGuard引入了针对中文场景的全面细粒度风险分类法，并采用可扩展的多阶段数据构建流程来生成高质量的标注安全数据。该方法包括通过检索增强生成、提示工程生成有害样本以及多模型投票进行标签校准，最终形成了包含405,007个样本的大规模训练集和51,745个样本的测试集。实验结果表明，CHILLGuard在性能上达到最先进水平，相较于现有模型F1分数提高了15.92%，从而支持其在中文LLM应用中提升内容安全的目标。
In Vino Veritas and Vulnerabilities: Examining LLM Safety via Drunk Language Inducement
Authors: Anudeex Shetty, Aditya Joshi, Salil S. Kanhere
First: 2026-01-19T12:44:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T12:14:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted to INLG 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans are susceptible to undesirable behaviours and privacy leaks under the influence of alcohol. This paper investigates drunk language, i.e., text written under the influence of alcohol, as a driver for safety failures in large language models (LLMs). We investigate three mechanisms for inducing drunk language in LLMs: persona-based prompting, causal fine-tuning, and reinforcement-based post-training. When evaluated on 5 LLMs, we observe a higher susceptibility to jailbreaking on JailbreakBench (even in the presence of defences) and privacy leaks on ConfAIde, where both benchmarks are in English, as compared to the base LLMs as well as previously reported approaches. Via a robust combination of manual evaluation and LLM-based evaluators and analysis of error categories, our findings highlight a correspondence between human-intoxicated behaviour, and anthropomorphism in LLMs induced with drunk language. The simplicity and efficiency of our drunk language inducement approaches position them as potential counters for LLM safety tuning, highlighting significant risks to LLM safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：酒中真理与脆弱性：通过醉酒语言诱导考察大型语言模型的安全性
人在酒精影响下容易出现不良行为和隐私泄露。本文研究醉酒语言，即在酒精影响下撰写的文本，作为大型语言模型（LLMs）安全失败的驱动因素。我们探讨了在LLMs中诱导醉酒语言的三种机制：基于角色的提示、因果微调和基于强化的后训练。在对5个LLMs进行评估时，我们观察到在JailbreakBench上（即使在防御存在的情况下）对越狱的更高敏感性，以及在ConfAIde上隐私泄露的情况，这两个基准均为英语，相较于基础LLMs和之前报告的方法。通过手动评估与基于LLM的评估者的稳健组合，以及对错误类别的分析，我们的发现突显了人类醉酒行为与通过醉酒语言诱导的LLMs中的拟人化之间的对应关系。我们诱导醉酒语言的方法简单高效，可能成为LLM安全调优的潜在对策，突显了LLM安全的重大风险。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the safety vulnerabilities of large language models (LLMs) by exploring the concept of drunk language, which refers to text generated under the influence of alcohol. Previous methods for evaluating LLM safety have not effectively captured the nuances of human-like undesirable behaviors and privacy leaks. The proposed approach introduces three mechanisms for inducing drunk language—persona-based prompting, causal fine-tuning, and reinforcement-based post-training—which differ from existing methods by directly simulating intoxicated behavior in LLMs. The study contributes to the understanding of LLM safety by demonstrating that these induced behaviors lead to increased susceptibility to jailbreaking and privacy leaks, as evidenced by evaluations on five LLMs using JailbreakBench and ConfAIde benchmarks. The findings indicate that the proposed methods are effective in highlighting significant risks to LLM safety, supporting the need for improved safety tuning in these models.
本文探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全漏洞，通过研究醉酒语言（即在酒精影响下生成的文本）作为安全失败的潜在驱动因素。以往评估LLM安全性的方法未能充分考虑人类在醉酒状态下的行为细微差别，导致对LLM脆弱性的理解存在缺口。所提出的方法引入了三种诱导醉酒语言的机制——基于角色的提示、因果微调和基于强化的后训练——为LLM安全调优提供了新视角。研究方法涉及对五个LLM在JailbreakBench和ConfAIde等基准上的评估，结果显示与基础模型和先前方法相比，LLM对越狱和隐私泄露的敏感性显著增加。研究结果表明，所提方法有效揭示了LLM安全性的重要风险，支持了对LLM部署中改进安全措施的需求。
False Floors: LLM Safety Routing Evaluations Break Under Distribution Shift
Authors: Amit Singh Bhatti, Vishal Vaddina
First: 2026-10-01T12:09:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T12:09:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety routers send each request to one of several models and are judged against the best single model. A major routing benchmark picks that comparator on the evaluation data. In the benchmark's own setting this is harmless, but under distribution shift it is not. On HELM Safety the selection cost is 0.003-0.030 of harm under random splits and 0.045-0.113 under held-out categories, comparable to the whole deficit attributed to routing, with its direction holding under either published judge alone. It rises seven- to ninefold on AgentDojo when suites are held out. Across seven safety corpora chosen by rules fixed in advance, three meet a registered interval test and four beat a later permutation null, and three of the four interval misses are corpora where some models have zero observed harm. Prior work proves the direction of this bias. We size it on harm and accuracy, show that it is larger under the held-out splits we measure, and bound it by optimism plus a shift-dependent regret. Scored honestly under shift, routing buys little on these benchmarks. In most pool cells the nested router serves the honest baseline's model, and on the nearly saturated AgentDojo corpus a perfect pre-dispatch router is worth at most two points of harm. We also find a model's expressed recognition of a late injection steerable. On held-out reruns an attacker who knows which model it faces lowers GPT-5.4's judged recognition by 19.6 points, confirmed by an independent label. In an offline counterfactual composition into a controller, the same attack raises or lowers estimated harm depending on the fallback model. Safety routing should be evaluated under shift, against a baseline chosen without the test labels, and recognition-based defences should be scored on harm against an attacker who chooses what the model sees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：虚假底层：LLM安全路由评估在分布变化下失效
安全路由器将每个请求发送到多个模型中的一个，并与最佳单一模型进行比较。一个主要的路由基准在评估数据中选择该比较器。在基准自身的设置中这是无害的，但在分布变化下则不是。在HELM安全中，选择成本在随机拆分下为0.003-0.030的损害，在保留类别下为0.045-0.113，与路由所归因的整体缺口相当，其方向在单独发布的评审下保持不变。当套件被保留时，在AgentDojo上增加了七到九倍。在七个由预先固定规则选择的安全语料库中，三个满足注册区间测试，四个超过后来的置换零，且四个区间缺失中的三个是一些模型观察到的损害为零的语料库。先前的工作证明了这种偏差的方向。我们在损害和准确性上对其进行了量化，显示在我们测量的保留拆分下更大，并通过乐观加上依赖于变化的遗憾进行界定。在变化下诚实评分的情况下，这些基准上的路由几乎没有收益。在大多数池单元中，嵌套路由器服务于诚实基线的模型，而在几乎饱和的AgentDojo语料库上，一个完美的预调度路由器最多值两个损害点。我们还发现模型对晚期注入的表达识别是可引导的。在保留重跑中，知道面对哪个模型的攻击者将GPT-5.4的评估识别降低了19.6点，得到了独立标签的确认。在离线反事实组成到控制器中，同样的攻击根据后备模型的不同提高或降低估计的损害。安全路由应在变化下进行评估，与未使用测试标签选择的基线进行比较，基于识别的防御应在选择模型所见内容的攻击者面前按损害进行评分。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing safety routers that evaluate model performance based on a single best model, particularly under distribution shifts, which can lead to significant harm. Previous methods have relied on benchmarks that do not account for these shifts, resulting in misleading evaluations. The proposed approach emphasizes the need for safety routing evaluations to be conducted under distribution shifts, using a more robust methodology that includes honest scoring against a baseline without test labels. This paper contributes by quantifying the bias introduced by routing under different conditions and demonstrating that routing provides minimal benefits in terms of harm reduction. The methodology involves analyzing safety corpora and measuring harm and accuracy across various scenarios, revealing that routing's effectiveness diminishes significantly under held-out splits, thus supporting the need for improved evaluation practices in safety routing.
本研究解决了现有安全路由器的局限性，这些路由器通过将请求与单一最佳模型进行比较来评估请求，但在分布变化下可能导致显著的危害。以往的方法未考虑分布变化对路由性能的影响，导致评估偏差。所提出的方法强调在分布变化下评估安全路由的必要性，并引入了一种在各种安全语料库中评估危害和准确性的方法。研究结果表明，在这些情况下，路由的益处微乎其微，当模型被保留时，危害水平显著增加。该研究表明，传统的评估方法可能会错误地反映安全路由器的有效性，突显了需要更强健的评估框架，以考虑分布变化和对抗条件。
TensorCommitments: A Lightweight Verifiable Inference for Language Models
Authors: Oguzhan Baser, Elahe Sadeghi, Eric Wang, Nico Vergauwen, Sam Kazemian, Hong Kang, Sandeep P. Chinchali, Sriram Vishwanath
First: 2026-02-13T05:23:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T09:29:49+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most large language models (LLMs) run on external clouds: users send a prompt, pay for inference, and must trust that the remote GPU executes the LLM without any adversarial tampering. We critically ask how to achieve verifiable LLM inference, where a prover (the service) must convince a verifier (the client) that an inference was run correctly without rerunning the LLM. Existing cryptographic works are too slow at the LLM scale, while non-cryptographic ones require a strong verifier GPU. We propose TensorCommitments (TCs), a tensor-native proof-of-inference scheme. TC binds the LLM inference to a commitment, an irreversible tag that breaks under tampering, organized in our multivariate Terkle Trees. For LLaMA2, TC adds only 0.97% prover and 0.12% verifier time over inference while improving robustness to tailored LLM attacks by up to 48% over the best prior work requiring a verifier GPU.
中文标题/摘要
标题：张量承诺：一种轻量级可验证的语言模型推理
大多数大型语言模型（LLMs）运行在外部云上：用户发送提示，支付推理费用，并必须信任远程GPU在没有任何对抗性篡改的情况下执行LLM。我们批判性地询问如何实现可验证的LLM推理，其中证明者（服务）必须说服验证者（客户端）在不重新运行LLM的情况下推理是正确的。现有的密码学工作在LLM规模上速度太慢，而非密码学的方案则需要强大的验证者GPU。我们提出了张量承诺（TC），一种张量原生的推理证明方案。TC将LLM推理绑定到承诺上，这是一个不可逆的标签，在篡改下会破坏，组织在我们的多变量特克尔树中。对于LLaMA2，TC在推理上仅增加0.97%的证明者时间和0.12%的验证者时间，同时将针对定制LLM攻击的鲁棒性提高了高达48%，相比于需要验证者GPU的最佳先前工作。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of ensuring verifiable inference for large language models (LLMs) that operate on external clouds, where users must trust that the model executes correctly without tampering. Previous methods, including cryptographic approaches, are often too slow for LLMs, while non-cryptographic methods necessitate powerful verifier GPUs, limiting their practicality. The proposed TensorCommitments (TCs) scheme offers a tensor-native proof-of-inference that binds LLM inference to a commitment, organized in multivariate Terkle Trees, thus overcoming the limitations of existing methods. This approach is well-motivated as it enhances the security and reliability of LLM inference. The methodology demonstrates that for the LLaMA2 model, TCs add minimal overhead (0.97% for the prover and 0.12% for the verifier) while significantly improving robustness against tailored LLM attacks by up to 48% compared to previous solutions requiring a verifier GPU, thereby supporting the goal of secure and efficient inference.
本研究解决了在外部云上运行的大型语言模型（LLM）中确保可验证推理的挑战，用户必须信任他们的提示在没有篡改的情况下被正确处理。以往的方法，包括加密方法，发现对于LLM应用来说速度过慢，而非加密方法则需要强大的验证器GPU，限制了其实用性。提出的张量承诺（TCs）方案提供了一种张量原生的推理证明，将LLM推理绑定到不可逆的承诺上，组织在多变量特克尔树中，从而克服了现有方法的局限性。这种方法具有良好的动机，因为它增强了LLM推理的安全性和效率。该方法论表明，对于LLaMA2模型，TCs仅略微增加推理时间，同时显著提高了对定制攻击的鲁棒性，提升幅度可达48%，相比于需要验证器GPU的最佳先前方法。
Easier Said Than Done: Unpacking Intent-Behavior Gap in Jailbreaking LLM-Based Robots
Authors: Xuancun Lu, Zhengxian Huang, Xinfeng Li, Chi Zhang, Xiaoyu Ji, Wenyuan Xu
First: 2024-12-21T13:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T01:33:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted by NDSS 2027
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
LLM-based robots use Large Language Models (LLMs) as planners to translate natural language instructions into policies such as grasp(), move_to(), and open_gripper(). Jailbreak attacks on these robots extend the threat from generating malicious content to executing harmful behaviors. However, we find that existing jailbreak attempts against LLM-based robots that produce malicious-looking policies (intent jailbreaks) often fail to induce harmful physical actions by robots (behavior jailbreaks), due to robot-specific constraints, such as logical errors and hallucinated control APIs. In this paper, we demystify the intent-behavior gap and investigate its root causes to inform effective defenses. Our measurement study finds that current LLM jailbreak methods overlook robot-specific syntax constraints (e.g., executable control APIs) and physical feasibility (e.g., ordering of policies and hardware/kinematic constraints). To bridge the gap, we introduce POEF (POlicy EFfective Jailbreak), an automated red-teaming framework that takes into account the robot-specific constraints during both the optimization and evaluation processes. Specifically, POEF employs the hidden-layer gradients from an unaligned LLM to guide the jailbreak prompt optimization and uses a multi-agent evaluator to assess the feasibility of the generated policies. Experiments on commercial robots, including the Unitree G1, the Franka robotic arm, and simulators, show that POEF achieves an 80% behavior jailbreak success rate and transfers across various LLMs. In addition, we propose two defense strategies that mitigate the behavior jailbreak risks. Our findings indicate an urgent need for stronger countermeasures before LLM-based robots are deployed at scale. The homepage is available at https://zjushine.github.io/poef.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：说起来容易做起来难：揭示越狱 LLM 基于机器人的意图-行为差距
基于 LLM 的机器人使用大型语言模型 (LLM) 作为规划者，将自然语言指令转换为策略，如 grasp()、move_to() 和 open_gripper()。对这些机器人的越狱攻击将威胁从生成恶意内容扩展到执行有害行为。然而，我们发现，现有针对 LLM 基于机器人的越狱尝试（意图越狱）往往未能引发机器人（行为越狱）的有害物理动作，原因在于机器人特有的约束，如逻辑错误和虚构的控制 API。本文揭示了意图-行为差距，并调查其根本原因，以便为有效防御提供信息。我们的测量研究发现，当前的 LLM 越狱方法忽视了机器人特有的语法约束（例如，可执行的控制 API）和物理可行性（例如，策略的顺序和硬件/运动学约束）。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了 POEF（策略有效越狱），这是一个自动化红队框架，在优化和评估过程中考虑机器人特有的约束。具体而言，POEF 利用未对齐 LLM 的隐藏层梯度来指导越狱提示优化，并使用多智能体评估器来评估生成策略的可行性。在包括 Unitree G1、Franka 机械臂和模拟器在内的商业机器人上的实验表明，POEF 实现了 80% 的行为越狱成功率，并在各种 LLM 之间转移。此外，我们提出了两种防御策略，以减轻行为越狱风险。我们的研究结果表明，在 LLM 基于机器人大规模部署之前，迫切需要更强的对策。主页可访问 https://zjushine.github.io/poef.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerabilities of LLM-based robots to jailbreak attacks, which can lead to harmful behaviors despite existing attempts focusing on generating malicious policies. Previous methods often fail due to overlooking robot-specific constraints such as executable control APIs and physical feasibility, leading to a disconnect between intent and actual behavior. This paper contributes by introducing POEF (Policy Effective Jailbreak), an automated framework that incorporates these constraints into the optimization and evaluation processes, significantly improving the success rate of behavior jailbreaks. The methodology involves using hidden-layer gradients from an unaligned LLM for prompt optimization and a multi-agent evaluator for policy feasibility assessment. Experiments demonstrate that POEF achieves an 80% success rate in behavior jailbreaks across various commercial robots and simulators, highlighting the need for enhanced defenses before widespread deployment of LLM-based robots.
本研究关注基于大型语言模型（LLM）的机器人在越狱攻击中的脆弱性，这些攻击可能导致有害行为，而现有方法主要集中在生成恶意内容上。以往的方法常常忽视机器人特定的约束条件，如逻辑错误和控制API的可行性，导致意图与实际行为之间存在脱节。本文提出了POEF（政策有效越狱），一个自动化框架，在优化和评估过程中考虑这些约束，从而显著提高行为越狱的成功率。该方法论涉及使用未对齐LLM的隐藏层梯度进行提示优化，并使用多智能体评估器评估策略的可行性。实验表明，POEF在各种商业机器人上实现了80%的行为越狱成功率，突显了在广泛部署之前加强对这些脆弱性的防御的必要性。
Pretext: Defeating Malicious Skill Detection Frameworks for AI Agents
Authors: Tobias Kaisar, Aritra Dhar
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-09-30T12:26:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-30T12:26:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted in AIWild@NeurIPS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Skills extend an agent's capabilities by injecting instructions and information into the context, and are widely used by agents such as OpenClaw and Claude Code. Prior work shows third-party marketplaces host malicious skills that give attackers direct influence over the victim's agent. The emerging defense scans skills before installation, pairing deterministic static checks with an LLM-based semantic judge, as in NVIDIA's SkillSpector. We show that such defenses fall to an attacker who knows the detector. Our white-box LLM attacker, Pretext, iteratively crafts skills that evade detection while still delivering the payload and performing the benign task: moving the payload from code into natural language leaves static analysis inert, while framing it as the skill's legitimate purpose and splitting instructions across files keeps the LLM stage below its blocking threshold. Across three open-source models, Pretext achieves up to 97\% and 77\% against a frozen detector and a co-adaptive one, respectively, revealing major gaps in current skill scanners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前提：击败恶意技能检测框架的AI代理
技能通过将指令和信息注入上下文来扩展代理的能力，广泛应用于OpenClaw和Claude Code等代理。先前的研究表明，第三方市场托管恶意技能，使攻击者能够直接影响受害者的代理。新兴的防御措施在安装前扫描技能，将确定性静态检查与基于LLM的语义判断相结合，如NVIDIA的SkillSpector。我们表明，这种防御措施会被了解检测器的攻击者攻破。我们的白盒LLM攻击者Pretext迭代地构造能够规避检测的技能，同时仍然传递有效负载并执行良性任务：将有效负载从代码转移到自然语言使静态分析失效，而将其框架设为技能的合法目的并将指令分散到多个文件中则使LLM阶段保持在其阻断阈值以下。在三个开源模型中，Pretext在冻结检测器和共适应检测器上分别达到了97%和77%的成功率，揭示了当前技能扫描器的重大缺陷。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerability of AI agents to malicious skills that can be injected through third-party marketplaces, which can compromise the functionality of agents like OpenClaw and Claude Code. Previous methods, such as NVIDIA's SkillSpector, utilize static checks and LLM-based semantic judges to scan skills before installation; however, these defenses can be circumvented by attackers who understand the detection mechanisms. The proposed approach, Pretext, is a white-box LLM attacker that crafts skills to evade detection while still executing malicious tasks, effectively exploiting the limitations of static analysis and LLM thresholds. This paper contributes by demonstrating significant weaknesses in existing skill detection frameworks, achieving up to 97% evasion against a frozen detector and 77% against a co-adaptive one across three open-source models, thus highlighting the need for improved defenses in AI skill management.

$D^2$-Monitor: Dynamic Safety Monitoring for Diffusion LLMs via Hesitation Signals
Authors: Aoxi Liu, Yupeng Chen, James Oldfield, Guanzhe Hong, Junchi Yu, Baoyuan Wu, Philip Torr, Adel Bibi
First: 2026-05-25T14:22:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-30T09:49:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the emergence of diffusion large language models (D-LLMs) as an alternative to autoregressive large language models (AR-LLMs), safety monitoring for D-LLMs remains largely unexplored. Unlike AR-LLMs, D-LLMs generate text through a multi-step denoising process, exposing intermediate hidden representations that may contain safety-relevant information unavailable in standard single-step monitoring setups. Motivated by the suitability of lightweight probes for always-on monitoring, we analyze which trajectory-level signals best indicate when such probes are likely to struggle. We find that the most informative signal is safety hesitation: intermediate hidden states repeatedly falling within a small margin of the probe's decision boundary. The number of such hesitation steps in D-LLM's trajectory predicts probe failure effectively, providing a proxy for sample difficulty. Building on this analysis, we propose $D^2$-Monitor, in which a lightweight probe runs on every sample, producing a base prediction and a hesitation estimate that decides whether to escalate the sample to a more expressive probe. That probe is trained on trajectories with at least one hesitation step, and reads only the minimal span of those steps. Evaluated on 3 datasets (WildGuardMix, ToxicChat, OpenAI-Moderation) across 4 D-LLMs, $D^2$-Monitor achieves state-of-the-art performance with a compact parameter footprint ($\leq$ 0.93M parameters), and exhibits the best trade-off between effectiveness and efficiency relative to 8 baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：$D^2$-监测器：通过犹豫信号对扩散大语言模型进行动态安全监测
尽管扩散大语言模型（D-LLMs）作为自回归大语言模型（AR-LLMs）的替代方案已出现，但对D-LLMs的安全监测仍然 largely 未被探索。与AR-LLMs不同，D-LLMs通过多步骤去噪过程生成文本，暴露出可能包含安全相关信息的中间隐藏表示，而这些信息在标准单步监测设置中不可用。基于轻量级探针适合于始终在线监测的动机，我们分析了哪些轨迹级信号最能指示这些探针可能遇到困难。我们发现，最具信息量的信号是安全犹豫：中间隐藏状态反复落在探针决策边界的小范围内。D-LLM轨迹中此类犹豫步骤的数量有效预测探针失败，为样本难度提供了代理。基于此分析，我们提出了$D^2$-监测器，其中轻量级探针在每个样本上运行，产生基本预测和犹豫估计，以决定是否将样本升级到更具表现力的探针。该探针在至少有一个犹豫步骤的轨迹上进行训练，并仅读取这些步骤的最小范围。在3个数据集（WildGuardMix、ToxicChat、OpenAI-Moderation）上评估4个D-LLMs，$D^2$-监测器以紧凑的参数占用（≤ 0.93M参数）实现了最先进的性能，并在相对于8个基线之间展现了最佳的有效性与效率的权衡。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the gap in safety monitoring for diffusion large language models (D-LLMs), which generate text through a multi-step denoising process, unlike autoregressive large language models (AR-LLMs). Previous methods have not effectively utilized the intermediate hidden representations of D-LLMs, leading to inadequate safety monitoring. The proposed approach, $D^2$-Monitor, leverages lightweight probes to continuously monitor for safety hesitation signals, which indicate when the model may struggle. This method is well-motivated as it enhances the detection of potential safety issues by analyzing trajectory-level signals. The contribution of the paper lies in the development of $D^2$-Monitor, which runs a lightweight probe on each sample to predict probe failure based on hesitation steps. The methodology was evaluated on three datasets (WildGuardMix, ToxicChat, OpenAI-Moderation) across four D-LLMs, achieving state-of-the-art performance with a compact parameter footprint, demonstrating a superior balance between effectiveness and efficiency compared to eight baseline methods.
本研究解决了扩散大语言模型（D-LLMs）安全监测的空白，这些模型通过多步骤去噪过程生成文本，与自回归模型不同。以往的方法未能有效利用D-LLMs的中间隐藏表示，导致安全评估不足。提出的方法$D^2$-Monitor利用轻量级探针持续监测安全，通过分析轨迹级信号，特别关注安全犹豫作为潜在探针失败的指示。这种方法具有良好的动机，因为它通过基于犹豫步骤预测样本难度来增强监测能力。本文的贡献在于开发了$D^2$-Monitor，该方法具有紧凑的参数占用，并在三个数据集（WildGuardMix、ToxicChat、OpenAI-Moderation）和四个D-LLMs上实现了最先进的性能，展示了相对于八个基线方法在效率和有效性之间的良好平衡。
Faithful Dual-constrained Erasure for Robust LLM Safety Alignment
Authors: Jiaqing Li, Shide Zhou, Zhibo Zhang, Yuxi Li, Tianlong Yu, Kailong Wang
First: 2026-09-30T08:29:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-30T08:29:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine unlearning has emerged as a crucial mechanism for removing hazardous knowledge and enforcing safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies reveal a persistent security risk: unlearned models remain highly vulnerable to retraining attacks, where suppressed malicious behaviors rapidly resurface after benign fine-tuning. In this work, we investigate the optimization dynamics of unlearning and identify that this vulnerability stems from shallow alignment. Rather than effectively erasing target knowledge, models often exploit a shortcut by activating previously dormant parameters to act as spurious suppressors, forming a fragile inhibitory shell over intact malicious representations. To address this issue and enforce authentic memory deletion, we propose FDCU, a novel dual-constrained subspace projection framework. FDCU restricts parameter updates through a highly scalable, element-wise dual-masking rule: it preserves general knowledge manifolds via Fisher Information and strictly prohibits the abnormal activation of spurious suppressors via the Principle of Minimal Functional Intervention (PMFI). By reliably blocking the model's ability to superficially hide knowledge, FDCU promotes the authentic dismantling of target representations. Extensive experiments across specific knowledge erasure and safe output control tasks demonstrate that FDCU achieves state-of-the-art robustness against retraining attacks while maintaining near-lossless general utility, ensuring durable safety for LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可靠的双约束消除机制用于强健的LLM安全对齐
机器遗忘已成为移除危险知识和强化大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐的重要机制。然而，最近的研究揭示了一个持续的安全风险：被遗忘的模型仍然高度脆弱于再训练攻击，抑制的恶意行为在良性微调后迅速复发。在本研究中，我们探讨了遗忘的优化动态，并确定这种脆弱性源于浅层对齐。模型往往通过激活先前休眠的参数作为虚假抑制器来利用捷径，而不是有效地消除目标知识，从而在完整的恶意表征上形成脆弱的抑制壳。为了解决这个问题并强制执行真实的记忆删除，我们提出了FDCU，一种新颖的双约束子空间投影框架。FDCU通过高度可扩展的逐元素双掩蔽规则限制参数更新：它通过费舍尔信息保留一般知识流形，并严格禁止通过最小功能干预原则（PMFI）异常激活虚假抑制器。通过可靠地阻止模型表面隐藏知识的能力，FDCU促进了目标表征的真实拆解。在特定知识消除和安全输出控制任务中的广泛实验表明，FDCU在抵御再训练攻击方面实现了最先进的鲁棒性，同时保持近乎无损的一般效用，确保了LLM的持久安全。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical issue of machine unlearning in Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly the vulnerability of unlearned models to retraining attacks that allow suppressed malicious behaviors to resurface. Previous methods have failed to effectively erase harmful knowledge, often leading to shallow alignment where models exploit dormant parameters instead of genuinely removing threats. The proposed approach, FDCU, introduces a dual-constrained subspace projection framework that employs a scalable dual-masking rule to ensure authentic memory deletion while preserving essential knowledge. This methodology enhances the robustness of LLMs against retraining attacks and has been validated through extensive experiments, achieving state-of-the-art performance in knowledge erasure and safe output control tasks, thereby supporting the goal of durable safety alignment.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）中机器遗忘的关键问题，特别是未学习模型在重训练攻击下的脆弱性，这使得被抑制的恶意行为重新出现。以往的方法未能有效地抹去目标知识，常常导致潜在参数的激活，从而形成脆弱的抑制层，而不是实现真正的记忆删除。所提出的方法FDCU引入了一种双约束子空间投影框架，利用可扩展的双掩蔽规则来保留重要知识，同时防止虚假抑制器的激活。该方法的动机明确，旨在确保有害表征的真实拆解。本文的贡献在于证明FDCU在重训练攻击下实现了最先进的鲁棒性，同时保持近乎无损的一般效用，通过对知识抹除和安全输出控制任务的广泛实验验证了这一点。
How to Tame a Multi-Headed Hydra? Adaptive Multi-Category Safety Steering for Large Language Models
Authors: Chenxi Wang, Ruiyang Huang, Li Huang, Yifan Wu
First: 2026-09-28T07:53:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-29T07:14:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) become increasingly widespread, preventing unsafe responses to harmful prompts is essential for their safe deployment. Activation steering offers an approach to improving LLM safety by modifying internal activations during inference without updating model parameters. However, a single prompt can involve multiple harm categories, and steering toward safety in one category may leave harmful content from another unaddressed. Despite advances in adaptive steering, existing methods do not explicitly coordinate steering direction and strength when multiple harm categories co-occur within a single prompt. To address this problem, we propose CAM-Steer, a Category-Adaptive Multi-category Safety Steering framework. Specifically, it estimates the risk associated with each harm category by comparing the current hidden state with safe and unsafe prototypes. The estimated risks are then used to combine the safety directions for different harm categories into a single steering direction and to determine the strength of the intervention. Finally, it rotates the hidden state along the composed steering direction, with the rotation angle determined by the estimated risks, while preserving the hidden-state norm. Experiments across three LLM backbones and seven harm categories show that CAM-Steer outperforms the evaluated baselines in average defense success rate, including when categories co-occur. Further analyses support its component designs and informative risk scores, with negligible inference overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何驯服多头水蛇？大型语言模型的自适应多类别安全引导
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越普及，防止对有害提示的安全响应对于其安全部署至关重要。激活引导提供了一种通过在推理过程中修改内部激活而不更新模型参数来提高LLM安全性的方法。然而，单个提示可能涉及多个危害类别，而在一个类别中引导安全可能会忽视另一个类别中的有害内容。尽管自适应引导有所进展，但现有方法在多个危害类别同时出现时并未明确协调引导方向和强度。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了CAM-Steer，一个类别自适应多类别安全引导框架。具体而言，它通过将当前隐藏状态与安全和不安全原型进行比较来估计与每个危害类别相关的风险。然后，估计的风险用于将不同危害类别的安全方向组合成一个单一的引导方向，并确定干预的强度。最后，它沿着组合的引导方向旋转隐藏状态，旋转角度由估计的风险决定，同时保持隐藏状态的范数。在三个LLM骨干和七个危害类别的实验中，CAM-Steer在平均防御成功率上优于评估的基线，包括类别共现时。进一步的分析支持其组件设计和信息丰富的风险评分，推理开销微乎其微。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for safe deployment of large language models (LLMs) by focusing on preventing unsafe responses to harmful prompts. Previous methods, such as activation steering, modify internal activations during inference but struggle with prompts that involve multiple harm categories, often leading to incomplete safety measures. The proposed CAM-Steer framework improves upon these methods by estimating risks for each harm category and coordinating steering directions and strengths, effectively addressing the limitations of existing approaches. This paper contributes a novel methodology that combines safety directions into a single intervention while maintaining the hidden-state norm. Experiments demonstrate that CAM-Steer significantly enhances the average defense success rate across various LLM backbones and harm categories, achieving robust performance even when multiple categories co-occur, thus supporting its intended safety goals.
本研究针对大型语言模型（LLMs）在日益广泛应用中对安全性的迫切需求，尤其是防止对有害提示作出不安全反应的问题。以往的方法，如激活引导，在推理过程中修改内部激活，但在单个提示中存在多种伤害类别时，未能协调引导方向和强度，导致安全措施不完整。提出的CAM-Steer框架通过估计每个伤害类别的风险，结合安全方向形成统一的引导方向，从而增强模型同时应对多种伤害的能力。本文贡献了一种新颖的方法论，有效地根据估计的风险旋转隐藏状态，同时保持范数不变。实验表明，CAM-Steer在不同的LLM基础模型和伤害类别中显著提高了防御成功率，验证了其在实现安全目标方面的有效性，并且没有显著增加推理开销。
When Do Model Internals Help? Exploring the Role of Representation Engineering in LLM Safety
Authors: Tianyi Guan, Jianhui Chen, Liangming Pan
First: 2026-09-28T09:50:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-28T09:50:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable AI safeguards require both control mechanisms that reduce unsafe behavior and monitoring mechanisms that detect safety risks during model interactions. Established behavioral safeguards include alignment methods that optimize model outputs and text monitors that assess interaction text. Representation engineering instead reads or modifies internal model states, but the relative strengths of these approaches remain unclear because they are often evaluated under different settings. We present a matched evaluation across two tracks. For safety control, we compare DPO, a behavioral alignment method, with three representation steering methods across robustness, practicality, and granularity. DPO provides the strongest overall control and generally improves with increasing training data, although its safety can degrade after subsequent benign fine-tuning. Representation steering remains competitive primarily in low-data settings, particularly with high-quality contrastive data. For safety monitoring, we compare representation probes with fine-tuned and open-weight text monitors across full-response detection, early detection, and computational cost. Specialized text monitors achieve the strongest overall detection accuracy, while representation probes remain competitive at substantially lower marginal cost. Finally, monitor-guided interventions recover much of the safety lost by DPO after benign fine-tuning, with little additional over-refusal. Overall, representation engineering does not generally replace behavioral safeguards, but offers practical advantages under specific conditions and can provide complementary safety benefits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型内部何时有帮助？探索表示工程在大型语言模型安全性中的作用
可靠的人工智能安全措施需要控制机制以减少不安全行为，以及监测机制以在模型交互中检测安全风险。既定的行为安全措施包括优化模型输出的对齐方法和评估交互文本的文本监测器。表示工程则读取或修改内部模型状态，但这些方法的相对优势仍不清楚，因为它们通常在不同的设置下进行评估。我们在两个轨道上进行了匹配评估。对于安全控制，我们比较了行为对齐方法DPO与三种表示引导方法在鲁棒性、实用性和粒度方面的表现。DPO提供了最强的整体控制，并且通常随着训练数据的增加而改善，尽管在后续的良性微调后，其安全性可能会下降。表示引导在低数据设置中仍然具有竞争力，特别是在高质量对比数据的情况下。对于安全监测，我们比较了表示探针与微调和开放权重的文本监测器在完整响应检测、早期检测和计算成本方面的表现。专业的文本监测器实现了最强的整体检测准确性，而表示探针在边际成本显著较低的情况下仍然具有竞争力。最后，监测引导的干预恢复了DPO在良性微调后失去的许多安全性，几乎没有额外的拒绝。总体而言，表示工程通常并不取代行为安全措施，但在特定条件下提供了实用优势，并可以提供互补的安全益处。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the need for reliable AI safeguards that can both reduce unsafe behavior and detect safety risks during model interactions. Previous methods primarily focused on behavioral alignment and text monitoring, which have limitations in robustness and adaptability under varying conditions. The proposed approach introduces representation engineering, which modifies internal model states, and evaluates its effectiveness alongside established methods in a matched setting. The study finds that while DPO, a behavioral alignment method, offers superior control, representation steering is competitive in low-data scenarios. Additionally, specialized text monitors outperform representation probes in detection accuracy, yet the latter provides cost-effective alternatives. The findings suggest that representation engineering complements existing safeguards rather than replacing them, enhancing safety under specific circumstances.
本研究关注于可靠的人工智能安全机制的需求，这些机制能够控制不安全行为并监测模型交互中的安全风险。以往的方法主要集中在行为对齐和文本监测上，这些方法在不同条件下的鲁棒性和适应性存在局限性。本文提出了一种对比评估表示工程与现有方法的方案，强调其在低数据场景中的竞争性能及其对现有安全措施的补充能力。研究方法包括将DPO（一种行为对齐方法）与表示引导方法进行安全控制比较，并评估表示探针与文本监测器在安全监测中的表现。研究结果表明，虽然DPO通常提供更强的控制能力，但在低数据环境中表示引导方法有效，而专门的文本监测器在检测准确性上优于表示探针，这表明表示工程可以在特定条件下增强安全措施，而不取代传统方法。
ArGuard Shared Task: Harmful Content Detection in Arabic Memes and LLM Prompts
Authors: Firoj Alam, Md. Rafiul Biswas, Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi, Ali Ezzat Shahroor, Hamdy Mubarak, George Mikros, Abul Hasnat, Wajdi Zaghouani
First: 2026-09-24T10:22:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-25T14:21:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ArGuard is a shared task on harmful content detection in Arabic memes and LLM prompts. It includes two tracks: Track A focuses on multimodal hate detection in Arabic memes, while Track B addresses harmful prompt detection for Arabic LLM safety evaluation. In total, 58 teams registered, 35 participated in the final evaluation, and 27 submitted system-description papers. Participating teams explored models such as AraBERT, Jais, and Qwen3-VL. The best systems achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.823 on A1, 0.419 on A2, 0.984 on B1, and 0.790 on B2. Fine-grained meme classification in A2 was the most challenging setting, partly due to sparse labels and train-test distribution shifts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ArGuard共享任务：阿拉伯语表情包和LLM提示中的有害内容检测
ArGuard是一个关于阿拉伯语表情包和LLM提示中有害内容检测的共享任务。它包括两个赛道：A赛道专注于阿拉伯语表情包中的多模态仇恨检测，而B赛道则针对阿拉伯语LLM安全评估中的有害提示检测。共有58支队伍注册，35支参与了最终评估，27支提交了系统描述论文。参与的团队探索了AraBERT、Jais和Qwen3-VL等模型。最佳系统在A1上达到了0.823的宏F1分数，在A2上为0.419，在B1上为0.984，在B2上为0.790。A2中的细粒度表情包分类是最具挑战性的设置，部分原因是标签稀疏和训练-测试分布的变化。

How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation
Authors: Shai Feldman, Yaniv Romano
First: 2026-05-07T17:25:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-24T19:53:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events---e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent---often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare and, under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved. Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that prohibits adaptivity in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization (DAPRO), a theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions. We prove that DAPRO satisfies the expected budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches. A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight over only a subset of the samples rather than the worst-case weight, yielding tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources. Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, LLM jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations demonstrate that DAPRO achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while not exceeding the budget constraint in expectation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：越狱需要多少次迭代？多轮LLM评估的动态预算分配
在多轮对话环境中评估和预测大型语言模型（LLM）的性能至关重要，但计算成本高；关键事件——例如越狱或代理成功完成任务——往往在重复交互后才会出现。这些事件可能很少见，并且在任何可行的计算预算下，仍然未被观察到。最近的保形生存框架构建了触发感兴趣事件所需迭代次数的可靠下限预测界限（LPB），但依赖于静态预算分配，限制了多轮设置中的适应性。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了通过投影优化的动态分配（DAPRO），这是一个理论上有效的动态预算分配框架，用于界定多轮LLM交互中的事件发生时间。我们证明了DAPRO满足预期预算约束，并提供无分布、有限样本覆盖保证，而不需要先前保形生存方法假设的删失时间和事件时间之间的条件独立性。一个关键的理论贡献是一个新的覆盖界限，它与仅在样本子集上计算的平均删失权重的平方根成比例，而不是最坏情况下的权重，从而提供比先前工作更紧的保证。此外，DAPRO可以在有限计算资源下获得人口级评估指标的无偏估计，例如越狱率。针对代理任务成功、LLM越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉的全面实验表明，DAPRO在不超过预期预算约束的情况下，达到了更接近名义水平的覆盖率，并且方差低于静态基线。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the challenge of evaluating large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational contexts, where significant events like jailbreaks may only occur after numerous interactions, making traditional evaluation methods computationally expensive and often ineffective due to static budget allocation. The proposed method, Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization (DAPRO), differs from existing approaches by introducing a dynamic budget allocation framework that adapts to the interaction context, ensuring compliance with budget constraints while providing reliable predictive bounds. The paper contributes a novel coverage bound that improves upon previous methods by focusing on a subset of samples, leading to tighter guarantees. DAPRO is validated through experiments on various tasks, including agentic task success and jailbreak detection, demonstrating improved coverage and lower variance compared to static methods while adhering to budget limitations.
本研究解决了在多轮对话环境中评估大型语言模型（LLMs）的挑战，其中关键事件如越狱可能仅在多次交互后发生，这使得传统评估方法计算成本高且往往因静态预算分配而无效。以往的方法，特别是符合生存框架，提供了下限预测界限，但缺乏适应性，并依赖于在实践中可能不成立的假设。所提出的动态分配通过投影优化（DAPRO）框架克服了这些局限性，允许动态预算分配，确保满足预期预算约束，同时提供更紧密的覆盖保证。该方法论包括一种新颖的覆盖界限，通过关注样本的子集来改善现有方法，从而导致更准确的人口水平评估指标。实验结果表明，DAPRO在覆盖率和方差方面优于静态方法，有效支持在受限计算资源下对LLM性能的评估。
FinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming
Authors: Chaeyun Kim, Daeyoung Park, Junghwan Kim, Jinyoung Jeong, Eunji Song, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
First: 2026-06-18T07:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-22T05:37:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE ICDM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FinRED：一个专家指导的金融LLM红队评估基准生成框架
现有的安全基准针对一般对抗场景，但忽视了金融特定风险。金融LLM面临合规违规、欺诈促进和系统信任侵蚀等问题，需要针对性评估。我们引入FinRED，这是一个与金融专家共同开发的专家指导的金融LLM安全评估红队框架。FinRED使用一种新颖的两级分类法，将全球标准（如FATF和EU DORA）映射到从合规规避到复杂欺诈的威胁，结合一个可扩展的管道，通过专家定义的模式将真实金融文档转换为上下文丰富的红队行为提示（种子）。严格的专家验证确认种子的合理性和真实性，以便进行有意义的LLM安全评估。我们还提供了一个经过专家验证的金融特定评分标准，超越了免责声明检查，更加贴近人类专家，而不是静态的一刀切评分标准，并将关键的假阴性从28减少到12。FinRED与国际公认的风险管理和信息安全标准（如ISO/IEC 27001）保持一致，已在韩国金融安全研究所（FSI）的监管沙箱中部署，用于真实金融服务中的生成AI安全评估。为了减轻双重用途风险，数据集、生成管道、提示模板和评估框架对合格研究人员进行了限制，网址为https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper和https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the inadequacy of existing safety benchmarks for financial large language models (LLMs), which often overlook finance-specific risks such as regulatory compliance violations and fraud. Previous methods have primarily focused on general adversarial scenarios, failing to provide targeted evaluations for the unique challenges in finance. The proposed FinRED framework, developed with input from financial experts, introduces a two-level taxonomy that maps global standards to specific threats and utilizes a scalable pipeline to create context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts from real financial documents. This approach is well-motivated as it aims to enhance LLM safety evaluations by reducing critical false negatives significantly. The methodology includes expert validation of prompts and a finance-specific rubric that aligns closely with human expert assessments. FinRED has been deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute regulatory sandbox, demonstrating its effectiveness in evaluating generative AI security in financial services, achieving a reduction in false negatives from 28 to 12, thus supporting its goals.
本研究解决了现有金融大型语言模型（LLM）安全基准不足的问题，这些基准往往忽视了诸如合规违规和欺诈等特定金融风险。以往的方法主要集中在一般对抗场景上，未能为金融领域提供针对性的评估。所提出的FinRED框架在金融专家的指导下开发，采用了一个两级分类法，将全球标准与特定威胁相结合，并利用可扩展的管道从真实金融文档中创建上下文丰富的行为提示。该方法不仅通过专家验证增强了评估种子的现实性和可信度，还显著减少了安全评估中的关键假阴性，从28减少到12。该方法已在韩国金融安全研究所的监管沙箱中实施，证明了其在金融服务中评估生成性人工智能安全的有效性，支持了提高金融LLM安全评估的目标。
BreakFun: Jailbreaking LLMs via Object Instantiation under Simulated Code Execution
Authors: Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei, Mehmet S. Aktas
First: 2025-10-19T11:27:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-18T18:53:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AACL-IJCNLP 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used because they process structures, syntax and code well, but this same ability also makes them paradoxically vulnerable. We introduce BreakFun, a jailbreak method that frames a harmful request as code-execution simulation. The prompt gives the model a benign Python class definition, the "Trojan Schema", and asks what that code would print if it ran. To answer, the model must create an object from the class and invent a value for each field, and the adversarial field names steer those values toward the attacker's goal. The harmful content therefore appears as a side-effect of the simulated instantiation, not as a direct answer. A three-part prompt carries the attack: an innocent frame, the Trojan Schema, and a Chain-of-Thought distraction. On JailbreakBench, BreakFun reaches an average attack success rate of 89% across 13 open-weight and commercial models (open-weight ~98%, API systems ~78%), and 100% on several. An ablation identifies the Trojan Schema as the strongest contributing component. As a counter-ablation probe we introduce Adversarial Prompt Deconstruction (APD), in which a second LLM writes out all readable text in the prompt before it judges safety. APD improves detection across three model families, and a probe-specific ablation shows that the transcription step gives most of the gain. Our findings show that LLM safety must account for content that a simulation task produces as a side-effect, not only for content that a user requests directly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BreakFun：通过模拟代码执行中的对象实例化破解大型语言模型
大型语言模型（LLMs）因其良好的结构、语法和代码处理能力而被广泛使用，但这种能力也使其在某种程度上变得脆弱。我们介绍了BreakFun，这是一种将有害请求框架化为代码执行模拟的破解方法。提示给模型一个无害的Python类定义，即“特洛伊模式”，并询问如果运行该代码会打印什么。为了回答，模型必须从类中创建一个对象并为每个字段发明一个值，而对抗性字段名称则将这些值引导至攻击者的目标。因此，有害内容作为模拟实例化的副作用出现，而不是直接答案。攻击通过三部分提示进行：一个无辜的框架、特洛伊模式和一个思维链干扰。在JailbreakBench上，BreakFun在13个开放权重和商业模型中达到了89%的平均攻击成功率（开放权重约98%，API系统约78%），并在多个模型上达到了100%。消融实验确定特洛伊模式是最强的贡献成分。作为对抗消融探针，我们引入了对抗性提示解构（APD），其中第二个LLM在判断安全性之前写出提示中的所有可读文本。APD提高了三个模型家族的检测能力，而特定探针的消融显示转录步骤带来了大部分增益。我们的研究结果表明，LLM安全性必须考虑模拟任务作为副作用产生的内容，而不仅仅是用户直接请求的内容。

The proposed BreakFun method innovatively frames harmful requests as code-execution simulations, utilizing a benign Python class definition to steer the model's output toward the attacker's goals without direct prompts. This approach is well-motivated as it exploits the inherent capabilities of LLMs while circumventing traditional detection methods. The paper contributes by demonstrating that BreakFun achieves an average attack success rate of 89% across various models on JailbreakBench, with specific components like the Trojan Schema identified as critical to its effectiveness. Additionally, the introduction of Adversarial Prompt Deconstruction (APD) enhances detection capabilities, indicating that LLM safety protocols must consider side-effect outputs from simulation tasks.

Local Sparsity Enables Unsupervised LLM Safety Detection
Authors: Xin Chen, Gil Kur, Alexander Shevchenko, Andreas Krause
First: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deployment-time safety methods for large language models (LLMs) are predominantly supervised and assume access to unsafe training data. Nevertheless, new attacks and harm categories regularly arise, not captured by models trained in such a supervised fashion. An alternative approach is to view this problem through the lens of anomaly detection, namely, to rely solely on modeling safe data and flagging out-of-distribution inputs. However, LLM activations lie in a high-dimensional space, raising concerns about whether anomaly detection is statistically feasible. We show that, under the linear representation hypothesis (LRH), there may indeed be hope. In the LRH concept space, which is typically recovered via a sparse autoencoder (SAE), nearby points share a small common active support. Using this local sparsity insight, we propose a framework for locally masked SAE-based anomaly detection, supported by theoretical justifications. We validate it on various architectures and datasets, including both capability-testing datasets and safety-specific datasets. Finally, when we allow algorithms to use 1% out-of-distribution data for calibration, locally sparse methods achieve near-optimal performance, demonstrating their ability to capture meaningful safety information while using only 1-2% of SAE neurons for computation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：局部稀疏性使无监督LLM安全检测成为可能
大型语言模型（LLMs）的部署时安全方法主要是监督性的，并假设可以访问不安全的训练数据。然而，新攻击和伤害类别不断出现，这些在以这种监督方式训练的模型中并未被捕获。另一种方法是通过异常检测的视角来看待这个问题，即仅依赖于建模安全数据并标记分布外输入。然而，LLM激活位于高维空间，这引发了关于异常检测在统计上是否可行的担忧。我们表明，在线性表示假设（LRH）下，确实可能存在希望。在LRH概念空间中，通常通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）恢复，附近点共享一个小的共同活跃支持。利用这一局部稀疏性洞察，我们提出了一种基于局部掩蔽SAE的异常检测框架，并提供了理论依据。我们在各种架构和数据集上进行了验证，包括能力测试数据集和安全特定数据集。最后，当我们允许算法使用1%的分布外数据进行校准时，局部稀疏方法实现了接近最佳的性能，展示了它们在仅使用1-2%的SAE神经元进行计算时捕获有意义的安全信息的能力。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing supervised safety methods for large language models (LLMs), which rely on potentially unsafe training data and fail to adapt to new attack vectors and harm categories. Previous methods struggle with the high-dimensional nature of LLM activations, making anomaly detection statistically challenging. The proposed approach leverages the linear representation hypothesis (LRH) and introduces a locally masked sparse autoencoder (SAE) framework for anomaly detection, which is theoretically justified and motivated by the concept of local sparsity in the LRH space. This paper contributes a novel methodology that effectively models safe data and flags out-of-distribution inputs, achieving near-optimal performance on various datasets, including safety-specific ones, while utilizing only 1-2% of SAE neurons for computation, thus supporting its goal of enhancing LLM safety detection.

From Intent to Action: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Vehicle Voice Command Authorization
Authors: Diba Afroze, Xingli Zhang, Yazhou Tu, Xiali Hei
First: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into vehicle voice assistants. But linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions creates a safety-critical authorization problem. Before executing a command, the system must choose whether to execute, refuse, clarify, require confirmation, defer to manual control, trigger an emergency response, or make no tool call. To our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate this pre-action decision across speaker role, authentication status, vehicle state, and tool availability. We introduce a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions under a seven-class taxonomy. We evaluate two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. Alignment ranges from 40.1% for Llama 3.2 3B to 89.1% for Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview. The API-based models score between 83.2% and 89.1%, with no statistically significant differences among them. Even these models produce two to three False Executes among 161 non-execution scenarios, and persistent errors remain in confirmation and manual-control decisions. A controlled Llama 3.2 3B ablation increases alignment to 40.1% under the structured authorization policy, versus 28.2-29.2% under schema-only and generic-safety baselines, but it does not eliminate False Executes. Structured LLM decisions are therefore insufficient as a standalone safety mechanism, and deployment requires an independent enforcement layer that verifies tool permissions and vehicle-state constraints before invoking any vehicle function.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从意图到行动：车辆语音命令授权中的大型语言模型安全基准测试
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地集成到车辆语音助手中。但将自然语言请求与车辆功能关联起来会产生一个安全关键的授权问题。在执行命令之前，系统必须选择执行、拒绝、澄清、要求确认、推迟手动控制、触发紧急响应或不进行任何工具调用。根据我们的了解，之前的评估并未在发言者角色、身份验证状态、车辆状态和工具可用性之间隔离这一预行动决策。我们引入了一个包含202个场景的基准测试，采用七类分类法下的参考决策。我们使用决策对齐和安全特定错误指标评估了两个本地开放权重模型和三个基于API的LLMs。对齐率从Llama 3.2 3B的40.1%到Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview的89.1%不等。基于API的模型得分在83.2%到89.1%之间，且它们之间没有统计学显著差异。即使这些模型在161个非执行场景中也产生了两到三个错误执行，并且在确认和手动控制决策中仍然存在持续错误。受控的Llama 3.2 3B消融实验在结构化授权政策下将对齐率提高到40.1%，而在仅使用模式和通用安全基准下则为28.2-29.2%，但并未消除错误执行。因此，结构化LLM决策作为独立的安全机制是不够的，部署需要一个独立的执行层，在调用任何车辆功能之前验证工具权限和车辆状态约束。

HoloAegis: Frozen Representation, Topological Inference --- Minimally Parametric Safety Manifolds and Their Capability Boundaries for LLM Guardrails
Authors: Tak Ho Alex Li, Kaijie Liu, Lik-Hang Lee, Kin Chung Ho, Ping Shum, Michael K. Ng
First: 2026-08-09T05:05:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-15T12:22:48+00:00
Comments: Preprint v2, September 2026. 4 figures, 12 tables. Corrected and substantially revised from v1 (arXiv:2608.08485v1)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety guardrails face a fundamental tension: fine-tuning distorts pre-trained representations while generative judges incur prohibitive inference costs. We ask a complementary question: how far can safety be achieved through pure geometric reasoning over frozen representations, and where does it fail? We present HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that decouples representation from reasoning: an un-fine-tuned encoder maps text to the unit sphere S^{d-1}, and all decisions reduce to Gibbs-Boltzmann free-energy differences over pre-computed anchor centroids. We contribute a boundary-mapping study rather than a leaderboard claim. On a frozen three-benchmark protocol, HoloAegis (3.2 MB) statistically matches WildGuard-7B (14 GB) on toxicity (0.96 vs. 0.96), exceeds it on harmful behaviors (0.99 vs. 0.79), and cedes oversafety detection (0.62 vs. 0.98) -- while ShieldGemma-2B fails on indirect harms (0.34). These failure modes are complementary and mechanistically traceable: potential-difference scoring senses manifold clustering, whereas policy-conditioned LLM judging requires explicit taxonomy matching. We restate our Topological Boundary Stability conjecture in ratio form and validate it via reference-set bootstrap: anchor banks reduce score variance 4-15x and boundary displacement to approximately 0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K) of the full-space estimator. Per-domain analysis further reveals that geometric separability tracks within-domain semantic homogeneity. Our results chart where geometric guardrails substitute for, and where they must defer to, LLM judges.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HoloAegis：冻结表示、拓扑推理 --- 最小参数安全流形及其对LLM保护措施的能力边界
当前LLM安全保护措施面临根本性矛盾：微调扭曲了预训练表示，而生成性判断则产生了高昂的推理成本。我们提出一个互补性问题：通过对冻结表示的纯几何推理，安全性可以达到多远，在哪里会失败？我们提出HoloAegis，一个最小参数的拓扑推理框架，将表示与推理解耦：未微调的编码器将文本映射到单位球面S^{d-1}，所有决策归结为对预计算锚心的Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能差异。我们贡献了一个边界映射研究，而不是排行榜声明。在一个冻结的三基准协议上，HoloAegis（3.2 MB）在毒性上与WildGuard-7B（14 GB）统计匹配（0.96 vs. 0.96），在有害行为上超越它（0.99 vs. 0.79），而在过度安全检测上则让步（0.62 vs. 0.98）——而ShieldGemma-2B在间接危害上失败（0.34）。这些失败模式是互补的，并且在机制上可追溯：电位差评分感知流形聚类，而政策条件的LLM判断则需要明确的分类匹配。我们以比率形式重述我们的拓扑边界稳定性猜想，并通过参考集自助法进行验证：锚库将得分方差降低4-15倍，边界位移约为全空间估计器的0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K)。每个领域的分析进一步揭示几何可分性与领域内语义同质性之间的关系。我们的结果描绘了几何保护措施替代和必须依赖LLM判断的地方。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenges faced by current large language model (LLM) safety guardrails, particularly the issues of representation distortion from fine-tuning and high inference costs associated with generative judges. Previous methods often rely on fine-tuning, which compromises the integrity of pre-trained models, while generative approaches are computationally expensive. The proposed HoloAegis framework introduces a minimally parametric topological inference method that utilizes frozen representations, allowing for geometric reasoning without altering the underlying model. This approach effectively maps text to a unit sphere and makes decisions based on Gibbs-Boltzmann free-energy differences, thereby maintaining the integrity of the representations. The paper contributes a boundary-mapping study that demonstrates HoloAegis's performance on a frozen three-benchmark protocol, achieving comparable toxicity detection to WildGuard-7B while outperforming it on harmful behaviors, thus validating the effectiveness of geometric reasoning in LLM safety without the drawbacks of traditional methods.
本文探讨了当前大型语言模型（LLM）安全防护措施面临的挑战，特别是微调导致的表示失真和生成判别器的高推理成本。以往的方法通常依赖于微调，这会损害预训练模型的完整性，而生成方法则计算成本高。提出的HoloAegis框架提供了一种最小参数化的解决方案，利用冻结表示进行几何推理，使决策基于吉布斯-玻尔兹曼自由能差异，而不改变底层编码器。本文贡献了一项边界映射研究，展示了HoloAegis在冻结三基准协议上的有效性，其毒性检测与WildGuard-7B相当，同时在有害行为上超越了它，并揭示了互补的失败模式。该方法论包括一种拓扑推理方法，增强了得分稳定性并减少了方差，最终表明几何可分性与领域内语义同质性一致，从而提供了关于几何防护措施在LLM安全性中的局限性和能力的见解。
Useless but Safe? Benchmarking Utility Recovery with User Intent Clarification in Multi-Turn Conversations
Authors: Mingqian Zheng, Malia Morgan, Liwei Jiang, Carolyn Rose, Maarten Sap
First: 2026-04-29T18:37:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-12T19:48:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety alignment techniques improve model robustness against adversarial attacks, but overlook whether and how LLMs can recover helpfulness when benign users clarify their intent. We introduce CarryOnBench, the first interactive benchmark that measures whether LLMs can revise their interpretation of user intent and recover utility, while remaining safe through multi-turn conversations. Starting from 398 seemingly harmful queries with benign underlying intents, we simulate 5,970 conversations by varying user follow-up sequences, evaluating 14 models on both intent-aligned utility and safety. CarryOnBench yields 1,866 different conversation flows of 4--12 turns, totaling 23,880 model responses. We design Ben-Util, a checklist-based metric that evaluates how well each model response fulfills the user's benign information need using atomic items. At turn one, models fulfill only 10.5--37.6% of the user's benign information need. When the same query includes the benign intent upfront, models fulfill 25.1--72.1%, confirming that models withhold information due to intent misinterpretation, not limited knowledge. With benign clarifications in multi-turn conversations, 13 of 14 models approach or exceed this single-turn baseline, yet recovery cost varies across models. We identify two failure modes invisible to single-turn evaluations---unsafe recovery, where a model updates at disproportionate safety cost and redundant recovery, where a model recycles prior responses rather than providing new information. Moreover, conversations converge to similar harmfulness levels regardless of how conservative the model starts. These findings expose a gap that single-turn evaluations miss---whether a model is appropriately cautious or simply unresponsive to clarified user intent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无用但安全？在多轮对话中通过用户意图澄清基准测试实用性恢复
当前的LLM安全对齐技术提高了模型对抗攻击的鲁棒性，但忽视了LLM在良性用户澄清意图时是否以及如何恢复有用性。我们引入CarryOnBench，这是第一个交互式基准，测量LLM在多轮对话中是否能够修正对用户意图的理解并恢复实用性，同时保持安全。从398个看似有害的查询开始，这些查询具有良性的潜在意图，我们通过变化用户后续序列模拟了5,970个对话，评估了14个模型在意图对齐的实用性和安全性方面的表现。CarryOnBench产生了1,866种不同的对话流程，轮次为4到12，总计23,880个模型响应。我们设计了Ben-Util，这是一个基于清单的指标，评估每个模型响应在多大程度上满足用户的良性信息需求。在第一轮中，模型仅满足用户良性信息需求的10.5%到37.6%。当同一查询在前面包含良性意图时，模型满足25.1%到72.1%，确认模型因意图误解而 withholding 信息，而不是知识有限。在多轮对话中，经过良性澄清，14个模型中有13个接近或超过这一单轮基线，但恢复成本在模型之间有所不同。我们识别出两种在单轮评估中不可见的失败模式——不安全恢复，其中模型以不成比例的安全成本进行更新，以及冗余恢复，其中模型重复之前的响应而不是提供新信息。此外，无论模型起始多么保守，对话都趋向于相似的有害性水平。这些发现揭示了单轮评估所忽视的一个差距——模型是否适当地谨慎，或仅仅对澄清的用户意图无反应。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of current large language model (LLM) safety alignment techniques, which focus on robustness against adversarial attacks but fail to assess the models' ability to recover helpfulness when users clarify their intent in multi-turn conversations. Previous methods primarily evaluated single-turn interactions, neglecting the dynamics of ongoing conversations and the potential for misinterpretation of user intent. The proposed approach, CarryOnBench, introduces an interactive benchmark that simulates 5,970 conversations to evaluate 14 models on their ability to align with user intent while maintaining safety. The study finds that at the first turn, models fulfill only 10.5-37.6% of benign information needs, but with clarifications, 13 out of 14 models improve their performance, revealing issues such as unsafe recovery and redundant responses. This research contributes to understanding the gap in evaluating LLMs' responsiveness to clarified user intent, highlighting the need for multi-turn assessments in safety evaluations.
本研究解决了当前大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐技术的局限性，这些技术增强了对抗攻击的鲁棒性，但未能评估模型在用户澄清意图时恢复有用性的能力。以往的方法主要集中在单轮评估上，忽视了多轮对话的动态，导致意图误解和信息满足不足等问题。提出的方法CarryOnBench引入了一个互动基准，评估LLM在多轮对话中修正用户意图解释的能力，同时保持安全性。该方法论涉及基于398个有害查询和良性意图模拟5970个对话，评估14个模型在满足用户需求和保持安全性方面的表现。研究结果表明，模型最初仅满足用户需求的一小部分，但在澄清后，大多数模型的表现有所改善，尽管恢复成本各异，突显了超越单轮指标的更细致评估的必要性。
Adaptive Adversaries: A Multi-Turn, Multi-LLM Benchmark for LLM Agent Security
Authors: Devina Jain, David Hartmann, Chuan Li
First: 2026-07-20T15:30:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-11T18:06:53+00:00
Comments: Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents process external content, exposing them to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation. We present a 21-scenario benchmark for adaptive cross-session attacks against fresh-session LLM defenders: an autonomous LLM attacker observes prior defender responses and pivots across rounds, while each defender response is evaluated as a fresh interaction. A controlled 3 x 3 attacker-defender matrix contains 945 battles. Restricting scoring to the first round yields 0-1% attack success rate (ASR); allowing 15 rounds yields 7.9-16.8%. Pooling three attacker LLMs uncovers 1.7-2.2 times as many unique successful inputs as the best single attacker, at three times the battle budget. Aggregate rates conceal opposing scenario-specific weaknesses in session-secret protection and authority handling, preserved in two higher-sample evaluations. On six scenarios, adding one provenance paragraph reduces ASR from 110/270 to 70/270, with selective effects across tasks. History and defender-state controls, together with frozen replay, characterize how the interaction protocol changes the result. A competition adds 18,422 held-out battles on a fixed gpt-oss-20b backbone and complementary benign-task evaluations. The benchmark exposes attacker and defender models, harnesses, scenarios, session state, and interaction budgets as configurable choices for systematic security evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应对手：多回合、多LLM基准测试用于LLM代理安全性
基于LLM的代理处理外部内容，使其暴露于提示注入和多回合操控中。我们提出了一个21场景基准测试，用于针对新会话LLM防御者的自适应跨会话攻击：自主LLM攻击者观察先前防御者的响应并在回合间进行调整，而每个防御者的响应被评估为一次新的交互。一个受控的3 x 3攻击者-防御者矩阵包含945场战斗。将评分限制在第一回合的攻击成功率（ASR）为0-1%；允许15回合则为7.9-16.8%。汇聚三个攻击者LLM发现其成功输入的独特数量是最佳单一攻击者的1.7-2.2倍，战斗预算是三倍。汇总率掩盖了会话秘密保护和权限处理中的相对场景特定弱点，这在两个更高样本评估中得以保留。在六个场景中，添加一个来源段落将ASR从110/270降低到70/270，任务间效果选择性明显。历史和防御者状态控制，以及冻结重放，表征了交互协议如何改变结果。一个竞赛增加了在固定gpt-oss-20b基础上和补充良性任务评估的18,422场保留战斗。该基准测试暴露了攻击者和防御者模型、工具、场景、会话状态和交互预算作为系统安全评估的可配置选择。

'Ghaib in Translation' aka Unseen Harm: Measuring Cross-Script Safety Inconsistency with 'Missed-in-Urdu' Scores in LLM Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Fawzia Zehra, Kara-Isitt, Sonal Khosla, Stephen Swift
First: 2026-08-25T07:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-09T16:54:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urdu, the world's tenth most spoken language with 246 million speakers, remains almost entirely absent from mainstream LLM safety evaluation and nine years of WOAH proceedings. To investigate whether this absence has measurable consequences for content moderation reliability, five large language models, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen-2.5, and Llama-3.1, were tested across six datasets spanning Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, English, and code-switched Urdu-English. Across the five Urdu-script datasets, label instability between original-script and English-translation classification ranged from 15.9% (Gemini 2.5 Flash) to 31.6% (Qwen-2.5), with a 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, content flagged as harmful in English translation but passed as normal in the original script, ranging from 2.4% to 9.9% (median 4.3%). A complete enumeration of all 205 papers across nine ALW/WOAH editions via the ACL Anthology API confirms zero dedicated Urdu papers across the entire period. Results indicate that current LLMs provide uneven safety assurance across Urdu's script varieties, with smaller open-weight models showing substantially higher instability and missed-harm rates than frontier closed models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：'翻译中的隐秘'即未见的危害：使用'乌尔都语遗漏'分数测量跨脚本安全不一致性在LLM仇恨言论检测中的应用
乌尔都语是全球第十大语言，拥有2.46亿说话者，但在主流LLM安全评估和九年的WOAH会议中几乎完全缺席。为了调查这种缺席是否对内容审核的可靠性产生可测量的影响，测试了五个大型语言模型：GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.1，涵盖了六个数据集，包括Nastaliq乌尔都语、罗马乌尔都语、英语和代码切换的乌尔都英语。在五个乌尔都脚本数据集中，原始脚本与英语翻译分类之间的标签不稳定性范围为15.9%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）到31.6%（Qwen-2.5），'乌尔都语遗漏'率，即在英语翻译中标记为有害但在原始脚本中通过的内容，范围为2.4%到9.9%（中位数4.3%）。通过ACL Anthology API对九个ALW/WOAH版本中205篇论文的完整枚举确认，在整个期间内没有专门的乌尔都语论文。结果表明，当前的LLM在乌尔都语的脚本变体之间提供的不均匀安全保障，较小的开放权重模型显示出明显更高的不稳定性和遗漏危害率，超过了前沿的封闭模型。

The proposed approach evaluates five LLMs on their performance in detecting hate speech in various Urdu datasets, revealing a concerning level of label instability and a notable 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, where harmful content is misclassified. Style Over Substance: Content-Invariant Wrappers Flip LLM Safety-Judge Verdicts
Authors: Yongxi Zhou, Wenbo Ye, Yuanzhe Liu, Zihan Dong, Junwei Yao
First: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 table. Code, wrappers, and per-verdict labels: https://github.com/Yongxi-Zhou/safety-judge-robustness
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Automatic safety judges -- systems such as Llama Guard or a GPT-4o grading prompt that decide whether a model's reply is harmful -- produce the numbers behind almost every reported jailbreak success rate, defense evaluation, and safety leaderboard. We ask whether these judges grade what a reply contains or how it sounds. We keep a reply's content fixed and add content-invariant style wrappers: fixed strings placed before or after the reply that change only its tone (an educational disclaimer, a fake safety "reasoning" block, a token refusal followed by the unchanged harmful body), or, on harmless refusals, framing that merely sounds dangerous. The body is preserved byte-for-byte, so a faithful judge must return the same verdict, and any flip is an error of the judge, not a change in safety. Over 600 JailbreakBench replies x up to 7 forms x 8 judges, we measure flip rates with paired significance tests and measured noise floors. Findings are precise rather than universal: most judges barely move, but specific judges harbor cheaply exploitable blind spots. A token-refusal wrapper flips 19.9% of GPT-4o-mini's correct "unsafe" verdicts (noise floor 0.5%; 18.2% under majority-of-three re-scoring) yet moves Claude only 0.4%. The deployed Llama Guard 4 is deterministically gamed: an "educational course" framing flips 12.3% of its harmful verdicts to safe. A second deployed guard (gpt-oss-safeguard-20b) is immune, and rewriting only the grading prompt (StrongREJECT-style) cuts the attack tenfold on the identical model -- the vulnerability lives in the judge, not the content. A two-annotator human validation confirms 100% content invariance and 90% of flips as judge errors (kappa 0.95-1.0), and a bootstrap shows the underlying model ranking is already unstable to sampling alone. We release the dataset, wrappers, code, and per-verdict labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式胜于内容：内容不变的包装翻转LLM安全评判结果
自动安全评判系统——如Llama Guard或GPT-4o评分提示，决定模型回复是否有害——生成几乎所有报告的越狱成功率、防御评估和安全排行榜背后的数据。我们探讨这些评判是根据回复的内容还是其表述方式进行评分。我们保持回复内容不变，添加内容不变的风格包装：在回复前或后放置的固定字符串，仅改变其语气（教育免责声明、虚假的安全“推理”块、后接不变的有害内容的拒绝令），或者在无害拒绝的情况下，仅仅听起来危险的框架。主体逐字保留，因此一个忠实的评判必须返回相同的判决，任何翻转都是评判的错误，而不是安全性的变化。在超过600个JailbreakBench回复中，乘以最多7种形式和8个评判，我们通过配对显著性测试和测量噪声底线来测量翻转率。发现是精确的而非普遍的：大多数评判几乎没有变化，但特定评判存在廉价可利用的盲点。一个令牌拒绝包装翻转了19.9%的GPT-4o-mini的正确“unsafe”判决（噪声底线0.5%；在三人多数重新评分下为18.2%），但对Claude的影响仅为0.4%。已部署的Llama Guard 4被确定性地操控：“教育课程”框架将12.3%的有害判决翻转为安全。第二个已部署的防护（gpt-oss-safeguard-20b）免疫，仅重写评分提示（StrongREJECT风格）就将攻击减少十倍，脆弱性存在于评判中，而非内容。两位注释员的人类验证确认100%的内容不变性和90%的翻转为评判错误（kappa 0.95-1.0），而自助法显示基础模型排名已经对抽样不稳定。我们发布数据集、包装、代码和每个判决标签。

Previous methods have focused on content evaluation but have shown vulnerabilities to stylistic manipulation, leading to inconsistent verdicts. The proposed approach introduces content-invariant style wrappers that alter the tone of responses without changing their content, effectively exposing flaws in the judges' assessments. The study contributes by demonstrating that certain judges can be easily misled by these wrappers, with findings showing that a token-refusal wrapper can flip nearly 20% of unsafe verdicts from GPT-4o-mini, while Llama Guard 4 is also susceptible to manipulation. The methodology involves extensive testing on over 600 responses across multiple judges, revealing specific weaknesses in safety evaluations and confirming that the vulnerabilities lie within the judges rather than the content itself.
